NarSil 1.4.0-236-g4e7664e4c seed catalog
Seeds 1 to 100, levels at 50 ft to 600 ft, 3 visits each
A seed gives the same levels only on the same game, version and data.  Each level is shown as
for a character diving straight down: artefacts found and uniques killed in a real game
change what later levels hold.
